This paper presents a metaheuristic approach using Iterated Local Search (ILS) and Simulated Annealing (SA) to optimize appliance start times, while considering appliance operating durations, power consumption, inverter limit, battery state of charge constraints, and solar generation forecasts. Unlike most existing work, the scheduling is extended beyond a single day to accommodate unfinished tasks from previous days (spillover), ensuring operational continuity and enabling sequential operation across multiple days. Experimental results show that the sequential multi-day scheduling framework effectively manages system constraints while ensuring user convenience under exclusive solar generation. These findings also open opportunities for future research on multi-objective trade-offs between investment in equipment of various sizes, return on that investment, and user satisfaction.

</details>


### [137] [Evaluation Sovereignty in Metadata-Driven Classification: A Multi-Track Framework for Weakly Supervised Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13436)
*Raymond Vasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨了机器学习评估中标签权威性对性能度量的影响,指出在大规模元数据系统中,模型在弱监督标签下的高性能可能反映的是与标注流程的对齐程度,而非真实预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习评估通常被视为中立的测量过程,但在实际运营信息系统中,评估结果往往受标签生成过程影响。特别是在大规模元数据驱动系统中,标签常常不完整、不一致或弱监督,这使得性能评估的有效性受到质疑。

Method: 论文引入"评估主权"(evaluation sovereignty)概念,定义为性能指标在多大程度上独立于标签权威和监督机制;提出多轨道评估框架,通过系统变化训练和评估标签源来测试模型;使用大规模科学元数据的层次多标签分类任务进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明,在操作性("银标准")评估下表现良好的模型,在独立("金标准")评估下性能大幅下降,尤其是细粒度分类任务。例如Micro-F1从约0.54降至0.03。但基于排序的指标仍高于基线,揭示了潜在模型信号与分类有效性之间的分歧。

Conclusion: 常见性能指标可能反映的是与标注过程的对齐程度,而非真实预测能力。因此需要将评估有效性重新概念化为由标签治理塑造的系统级属性,并提供实用的方法论来审计弱监督环境下运行的智能系统。

Abstract: Evaluation in machine learning is typically treated as a neutral measurement process. However, in operational information systems, evaluation outcomes are often conditioned by the processes used to generate labels. This paper does not seek to improve classification performance. Instead, it examines the validity of performance measurement under differing label-authority regimes. This issue is particularly relevant in large-scale metadata-driven systems, where labels are often incomplete, inconsistent, or weakly supervised.
  We introduce evaluation sovereignty, defined as the degree to which performance metrics are independent of label authority and supervision regime, and propose a multi-track evaluation framework that systematically varies training and evaluation label sources. Using hierarchical multi-label classification on large-scale scientific metadata, we demonstrate that models exhibiting strong performance under operational ("silver") evaluation degrade substantially under independent ("gold") evaluation, particularly for fine-grained classification. For example, Micro-F1 decreases from approximately 0.54 to 0.03. Notably, ranking-based metrics remain above baseline, revealing a divergence between latent model signal and classification validity.
  These findings suggest that commonly reported performance metrics may reflect alignment with labeling processes rather than true predictive capability. We therefore reconceptualize evaluation validity as a system-level property shaped by label governance and provide a practical methodology for auditing intelligent systems operating under weak supervision.

</details>


### [138] [Why Sampling Is Not Choosing: Intentionality, Agency, and Moral Responsibility in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13441)
*Joseph Keshet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证大型语言模型(LLMs)不具备真正的代理能力或道德主体资格。作者认为道德责任需要基于内在意向性和自我归属行为的承诺承担型代理，而LLM的运作完全由从数据中学习的概率输入-输出映射决定，其表面意向性是派生的而非内在的，输出既不被拥有为承诺也不受理由指导。随机采样引入的变异性不构成选择或创作。作者回应了来自意向立场、功能主义、兼容主义和模型输出中的道德推理等反驳，认为这些都不能证明真正的代理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型(LLM)的最新进展，学界有声音声称这些系统展现出了代理能力或可被视为道德主体。本文旨在批驳这些归因，认为现有LLM并不具备真正的代理能力或道德主体资格。

Method: 作者采用哲学分析方法，深入剖析道德责任的构成要件。作者论证真正的道德责任需要一种「承诺承担型代理」(commitment-bearing agency)，其基础是内在意向性和自我归属的行为——这构成与责任相关的自由意志形式。

Result: 尽管LLM能生成连贯且可被规范评估的输出，但其运作机制完全由从数据中学习的概率输入-输出映射所刻画。其表面上的意向性是派生而非内在的，输出既不被拥有为承诺，也不受理由指导。随机采样引入的变异性不构成真正的选择或创作。

Conclusion: 作者进一步回应了来自意向立场、功能主义、兼容主义以及模型输出包含道德推理等观点的反驳，论证这些都不足以确立真正的代理能力。道德责任的核心要求——基于内在意向性的承诺承担型代理——是LLM无法满足的。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have prompted claims that such systems exhibit agency or qualify as moral agents. This paper argues that these attributions are misguided. We maintain that moral responsibility requires commitment-bearing agency grounded in intrinsic intentionality and self-attributed action, and that such agency constitutes the form of free will relevant to responsibility. Although LLMs generate coherent and normatively evaluable outputs, their operation is fully characterized by probabilistic input-output mappings learned from data. Their apparent intentionality is derived rather than intrinsic, and their outputs are neither owned as commitments nor guided by reasons. Variability introduced by stochastic sampling does not amount to choice or authorship. We address objections from the intentional stance, functionalism, compatibilism, and the presence of moral reasoning in model outputs, arguing that none suffice to establish genuine agency.

</details>


### [139] [CloudCons: A Comprehensive End-to-End Benchmark for Cloud Resource Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13513)
*Xiaobin Zhang,Lefei Shen,Mouxiang Chen,Zhuo Li,Hongkai Li,Han Fu,Jianling Sun,Xiaoxue Ren,Chenghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CloudCons是一个用于评估云资源整合中预测模型决策效用的基准测试。研究发现，尽管基础模型在零样本预测精度上表现更好，但这优势并不必然转化为更好的决策效用。关键发现是预测分位数选择是平衡资源效率和服务可靠性的关键杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据中心因保守的资源配置导致利用率低下，现有的预测模型基准测试仅关注预测误差指标，未验证模型的实际决策效用，导致这些先进模型对下游任务的实际价值存疑。

Method: 提出CloudCons基准测试，涵盖华为云、微软Azure和Google Borg的多样化工作负载（昼夜节律、脉冲式突发、高频噪声）。对统计模型、深度学习模型和基础模型进行广泛评估。

Result: 基础模型虽然展示出更优的零样本预测精度，但这一优势并不自动转化为更好的决策效用。预测分位数选择是影响决策效果的关键因素。

Conclusion: 预测精度优势不必然带来决策效用提升。为实现资源效率与服务可靠性的平衡，需系统性校准预测分位数选择，为实际部署提供可操作的指导方针。

Abstract: Driven by conservative over-provisioning to guarantee service reliability, resource utilization in cloud data centers remains at low levels. To mitigate this, the forecast-then-optimize paradigm has emerged to optimize consolidation by anticipating future demands. While emerging time series foundation models promise to enhance this paradigm through zero-shot generalization, existing benchmarks focus solely on prediction error metrics. The actual decision utility of these advanced models remains unverified, rendering their practical value for downstream tasks uncertain. To bridge this gap, we propose CloudCons, a comprehensive end-to-end benchmark designed to evaluate forecasting models within the specific context of cloud resource consolidation. We build high-quality datasets that cover diverse workloads from Huawei Cloud, Microsoft Azure, and Google Borg, capturing distinct service characteristics ranging from synchronized diurnal rhythms to stochastic, pulse-like bursts and high-frequency noise. We conduct an extensive evaluation of statistical, deep learning, and foundation models. Our experiments reveal a pivotal finding: while foundation models demonstrate superior zero-shot forecasting accuracy, this advantage does not inherently translate into better decision utility. Of practical significance, we systematically analyze how the selection of predictive quantiles acts as a critical lever. We provide actionable guidelines for calibrating these selections to balance the trade-off between resource efficiency and service reliability, offering vital insights for real-world deployment decisions.

</details>


### [140] [Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Retrieval for Long-Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13550)
*Hoin Jung,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UMG-RAG提出一种无需训练的超轻量级混合检索框架，通过将块粒度视为查询相关的可靠性估计来解决RAG中检索单元粒度选择的问题。核心思想是利用不同粒度的密集和稀疏检索器作为互补专家，根据分布熵估计可靠性并进行动态融合。同时提出UMGP-RAG变体，通过细粒度命中定位证据但返回更宽泛的父块以保证局部连贯性。实验表明该方法在问答任务上提升了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖检索质量，但现有方法在大块（保留上下文但含无关内容）和小块（紧凑但缺乏语义/词汇线索难以可靠检索）之间存在两难抉择。

Method: 1) 使用现成的密集检索器和稀疏检索器作为不同粒度的互补专家；2) 将每个专家-粒度分数列表转换为证据分布；3) 从分布熵估计可靠性；4) 根据查询相关的语义、词汇和粒度置信度融合候选结果。UMGP-RAG进一步利用细粒度命中定位相关证据，但返回更广泛的非冗余父块以保证局部连贯性。

Result: 在问答基准测试上的实验表明，不确定性感知融合和父块提升能提升生成质量，同时保持轻量级、即插即用的检索流程。

Conclusion: 通过将块粒度选择问题转化为查询相关的可靠性估计问题，UMG-RAG有效解决了RAG中检索粒度的挑战。该方法无需训练、可扩展性强，为构建高质量RAG系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) depends critically on the quality and granularity of retrieved evidence. Large retrieval units preserve context but often introduce irrelevant content, which can dilute answer bearing evidence and worsen long context utilization. Fine-grained units are more compact, but they may be difficult to retrieve reliably because short chunks can lack semantic, lexical, or bridging cues needed to match the query. We propose Uncertainty-aware Multi-Granularity RAG (UMG-RAG), a training-free hybrid retrieval framework that treats chunk granularity as query-specific reliability estimation. Instead of training a new retriever or modifying the generator, UMG-RAG uses existing dense and sparse retrievers as complementary experts across multiple chunk granularities. For each query, it converts each expert-granularity score list into an evidence distribution, estimates reliability from distribution entropy, and fuses candidates according to query-specific semantic, lexical, and granularity confidence. We further introduce UMGP-RAG, a parent promotion variant that uses fine-grained hits to locate relevant evidence while returning broader non-redundant parent chunks for local coherence. Experiments on question answering benchmarks show that uncertainty-aware fusion and parent promotion improve generation quality while maintaining a lightweight, plug-and-play retrieval pipeline.

</details>


### [141] [Is It You or Your Environment? A Bayesian Inference Framework for Genomically-Anchored Personalized Physiological Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13556)
*Aruna Dey,Suraj Biswas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用基因组特征作为个性化贝叶斯先验来解决个性化健康AI系统的"冷启动"问题。该方法将基因组信息作为外生遗传锚点，在收集任何行为数据前即可初始化生理基线的贝叶斯信念状态，并通过随时间衰减的权重从基因组主导推断过渡到经验基线主导推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化健康AI系统面临根本性的"冷启动"问题：用于生理解读的机器学习模型需要数周的个体行为数据才能区分体质性变异与环境驱动性偏差。在获得足够的行为观测之前，模型无法建立个体化的基线判断。

Method: 1）利用基因组概貌作为外生遗传锚点，生成领域知情的个性化先验（固定于受孕时，对反向因果免疫）；2）建立贝叶斯信念状态Ĝ = μ + Σ(βᵢ·gᵢ)，其中βᵢ为GWAS衍生效应大小，gᵢ为风险等位基因计数；3）定义非体质性偏差δ = P - Ĝ以分离环境信号与体质基线；4）实现动态衰减机制Ĝₜ = w(t)Ĝ_genomic + [1-w(t)]P̄ₜ，实现从基因组主导到经验主导的推断过渡；5）在六个生理领域开发架构并按证据强度分级基因组先验（区分FTO/FADS1/2/FKBP5等稳健锚点与SLC6A4/MAOA/DRD2等争议候选基因）。

Result: 同一生理测量值可因个体基因组先验不同而产生截然不同的假说。例如，相同的心率变异性（HRV）55ms值，对应先验预测80ms的个体产生抑制假说，对应先验预测30ms的个体产生增强假说——这种假说反转在没有个性化锚点的情况下不可能实现。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个基于因果推断和贝叶斯先验设计的框架，通过基因组锚点解决个性化生理监测的冷启动问题。定义了四项部署约束：证据分级先验、动态衰减、祖先匹配效应大小、以及归因而非确定性输出。同时明确了关联分析、孟德尔随机化和个体 token 因果之间的推理边界。

Abstract: Personalized health AI systems face a fundamental cold-start problem: machine learning models for physiological interpretation require weeks of individual behavioral data before they can distinguish constitutional variation from environmentally driven deviation. We propose a solution grounded in causal inference and Bayesian prior design. An individual's genomic profile serves as an exogenous genetic anchor -- a domain-informed, personalized prior that is fixed at conception, immune to reverse causation, and available before a single behavioral observation is collected. The anchor initializes a Bayesian belief state over an individual's physiological set point G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i), where beta_i are GWAS-derived effect sizes and g_i are risk-allele counts. Each incoming physiological measurement P produces a non-constitutional deviation delta = P - G-hat that separates the signal attributable to environment and state from the constitutionally fixed baseline. As behavioral data accrue, the prior decays according to G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t, transitioning from genome-dominated to empirical-baseline-dominated inference. The same observed HRV of 55 ms generates a suppression hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 80 ms, and an enhancement hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 30 ms -- a reversal impossible without a personalized anchor. We develop this architecture across six physiological domains, grading genomic priors by evidence strength, distinguishing robustly replicated anchors (FTO, FADS1/2, FKBP5) from contested candidate genes (SLC6A4, MAOA, DRD2). We address the inference boundary between association, Mendelian randomization, and individual token causation, and define four constraints for deployment: evidence-graded priors, dynamic decay, ancestry-matched effect sizes, and attribution rather than deterministic output.

</details>


### [142] [Reward Modeling for Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13598)
*King Yeung Tsang,Zihao Zhao,Vishal Venkataramani,Haizhou Shi,Zixuan Ke,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OrchRM，一个自监督的多智能体系统编排奖励建模框架，通过利用中间执行产物构建胜负对来训练Bradley-Terry奖励模型，实现无需人工标注的编排质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型构建的多智能体系统需要有效编排，但训练编排器面临监督信号有限和计算成本高昂的问题。现有方法依赖昂贵的子智能体rollout，缺乏高效的训练和测试时扩展方案。

Method: OrchRM利用多智能体执行过程中的中间产物构造win-lose pairs，用于Bradley-Terry奖励模型训练。与现有框架不同，OrchRM直接在编排层面操作，无需对子智能体进行昂贵的rollout，从而实现高效的奖励引导式编排器训练和多智能体系统测试时扩展。

Result: 在token使用量上提升10倍训练效率，同时在准确率上提升8%的测试时扩展性能。这些改进在数学推理、网页问答和多跳推理等多个领域均表现一致，验证了编排级奖励建模作为多智能体编排可扩展方向的可行性。

Conclusion: OrchRM证明了自监督的编排级奖励建模是实现高效多智能体系统编排训练和测试时扩展的有效途径，为构建更强大的多智能体系统提供了新的可扩展方向。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) require effective orchestration to coordinate specialized agents, yet training such orchestrators is hindered by limited supervision and high computational cost. We propose Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM), a self-supervised framework for evaluating orchestration quality without human annotations. OrchRM leverages intermediate artifacts from multi-agent executions to construct win-lose pairs for Bradley-Terry reward model training. Unlike existing MAS test-time scaling and orchestrator training frameworks that rely on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient and high-performing reward-guided orchestrator training and MAS test-time scaling. OrchRM improves training efficiency by up to 10x in token usage while improving MAS test-time scaling performance by up to 8% in accuracy. These gains consistently transfer across multiple domains, including mathematical reasoning, web-based question answering, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating orchestration-level reward modeling as a scalable direction for robust multi-agent orchestration. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/OrchRM.

</details>


### [143] [A Three-Layer Framework for AI in Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13566)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AI在科学发现中的三层视图：搜索检索(L1)、定性推理的模型形成(L2)、执行优化(L3)，核心主张L2是最重要但最不发达的能力，并通过三个案例说明结构洞思维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI科学发现的讨论被搜索和执行两种能力主导，但两者都无法充分捕捉科学发现的核心行为——模型的形成与演化。现有框架缺乏对概念创新的系统性理解。

Method: 提出三层分析框架：L1为LLM的搜索检索，L2为通过定性推理进行模型形成（核心创新），L3为执行优化。L2强调识别当前框架的结构性不足，在更广泛的表征空间中理解问题，而非通过试错。

Result: 通过三个案例验证框架：(1)陈省身对Gauss-Bonnet定理的内蕴证明，(2)Lyapunov函数解决Nesterov加速梯度收敛问题，(3)OpenAI于2026年自动证伪Erdős单位距离猜想。

Conclusion: Layer 2是AI辅助科学发现最关键但发展最不足的层次。搜索无模型形成困于固有框架，执行无概念修正仅放大现有形式。真正的发现需要识别结构性不足和缺失的概念对象。

Abstract: Current discussions of AI in scientific discovery are often dominated by two visible capabilities: search over existing knowledge and execution through optimization, simulation, and automation. Both are important, but neither fully captures the central act of discovery: the formation and evolution of models. This paper proposes a three-layer view of AI in discovery. Layer 1 is search and retrieval by large language models. Layer 2, as the main innovation of this paper, is model formation through qualitative reasoning: the capacity to recognize when a current framework is structurally inadequate and to understand the problem within a broader representational space, not through trial and error, but through structural insight into what is missing and where it can be found. Layer 3 is execution, optimization, and refinement. The main claim is that Layer 2 is both the most important and the least developed. Search without model formation remains confined to inherited frameworks, while execution without conceptual revision only amplifies an existing formulation. We illustrate Layer 2 reasoning through three case studies: S. S. Chern's intrinsic proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, the resolution of the Nesterov Accelerated Gradient convergence problem via Lyapunov functions, and the autonomous disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture by OpenAI in 2026. Each case exhibits the same structural signature: a framework that had become inadequate, a missing conceptual object, and a resolution found in an unexpected neighboring field.

</details>


### [144] [EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13662)
*Amy Xin,Jiening Siow,Junjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Fanjin Zhang,Jian Song,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出环境工程（environment engineering）作为自动驾驶科学发现的新范式，并推出EurekAgent系统，通过权限工程、工件工程、预算工程和人机交互工程四个维度设计智能体环境，在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得最先进结果，成本低于11美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型能力提升，自动驾驶科学发现的瓶颈正从设计智能体工作流程转向设计智能体环境。现有的智能体设计主要关注工作流（workflow），忽视了环境（environment）对智能体行为的塑造作用。

Method: 提出环境工程框架，从四个维度设计智能体环境：1）权限工程：边界执行和隔离评估；2）工件工程：文件系统和Git协作；3）预算工程：预算感知探索；4）人机交互工程：简化人工监督和干预。EurekAgent系统整合这四个维度。

Result: 在数学、内核工程和机器学习等多个任务上达到SOTA，包括26圆 packing 问题的新SOTA（成本低于11美元）。

Conclusion: 环境工程应成为开发可靠自动驾驶研究智能体的核心研究方向，开源代码和数据。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.

</details>


### [145] [Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13591)
*Ali Elahi,Barbara Di Eugenio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种协议，通过转换置信度信号使其跨模型可比，然后使用软投票或贝叶斯融合聚合，为多智能体辩论系统生成单一聚合置信度。实验表明该方法在保持F1稳定的同时，显著提高了置信度的区分度(AUARC)，并能恢复模糊任务上的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 置信度估计在NLP中用于可靠性保障、监督和下游决策任务，但现有方法无法为多智能体系统输出生成或评估置信度。先前工作在多智能体辩论(MAD)中使用置信度仅用于加权消息、触发辩论或校准单个智能体，从未将这些信号聚合成系统级置信度。

Method: 1) 提出三种协议生成最终答案及单一聚合置信度；2) 先将原始置信度信号转换使其跨模型可比；3) 通过软投票或贝叶斯融合方法组合；4) 评估了序列概率和自报告两种估计器，以及参数化和非参数化校准器；5) 在5个基准测试、4种任务类型、6个同质/异质辩论对上进行实验。

Result: 聚合置信度显著优于最佳单智能体和标准辩论基线(AUARC更高)；正确性(F1)保持稳定；恢复了MAD在模糊任务上的性能损失；校准可改善两种估计器的F1；AUARC对校准的依赖性较低。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功为多智能体辩论系统提供了可靠的系统级置信度估计，在保持正确性的同时显著提升了置信度的区分能力，且对不同模型能力和规模均适用。

Abstract: Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.

</details>


### [146] [EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13602)
*Harihara Muralidharan,Reema Baskar,Soo Hee Lee,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiBench是一个用于评估AI智能体在表观基因组学短时域分析中决策能力的基准测试，涵盖4种实验类型共106项评估。16个模型-框架组合在5,088条有效轨迹上的测试表明，即使表现最佳的GPT-5.5/Pi也仅达到45%的通过率，所有系统均未超过半数，表明当前AI在需要深入assay特异性科学判断的任务上仍有明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏可验证的基准来评估AI智能体是否能够从现实的表观基因组学工作流状态中做出明确定义的分析决策，并返回可确定性评分的答案。现有评估缺乏标准化和可重复性。

Method: 构建EpiBench基准测试，包含106项评估任务，涵盖CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化四种工作流程。测试16种模型-框架组合（model-harness pairs），生成5,088条有效轨迹进行评估。

Result: 所有系统均未通过半数评估。GPT-5.5/Pi表现最佳（45.0%, 95%CI: 36.3-53.7），其次为GPT-5.5/OpenAI Codex（39.9%）和Claude Opus 4.8 Max/Pi（39.0%）。性能在不同assay类型间存在差异，许多失败案例包含部分正确答案——智能体能找到正确文件并计算有用的中间结果。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统能够执行表观基因组学分析的基础操作（文件定位、初步计算），但在需要更深层次assay特异性科学判断的任务上存在明显短板，表明专业领域知识与推理能力的结合仍是挑战。

Abstract: We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.

</details>


### [147] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13604)
*Haochen Wu,Yi Hou,Shiguang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DoorDash部署的一个强化学习系统，通过学习延迟的操作结果信号（如配送速度、骑手利用率和商家拥挤度）来调整外卖配送市场中的调度目标权重。系统采用门店级策略，通过选择离散乘数来调整配送质量与批量效率之间的权衡，在保持生产可行性和运营安全约束的同时实现离线策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在三方市场（顾客、骑手、商家）的调度中，决策效果需要通过延迟的操作结果来评估。传统方法难以处理噪声、延迟和耦合的反馈信号，需要一种既能利用真实世界反馈又能保证生产可行性的方法。

Method: 1. 使用门店级策略从日志化的市场数据中学习，选择离散乘数来调整调度优化器的权衡；2. 采用集中式离线数据训练共享价值函数，去中心化门店级执行；3. 使用Double Q-learning目标和保守正则化器来减少分布外价值高估；4. 生产环境采用开关实验（switchback experiment）进行验证。

Result: 离线训练的策略在生产开关实验中成功提升了批量效率，降低了骑手侧时间成本，同时未降低客户感知的配送质量。

Conclusion: 真实经济与物流系统的世界反馈可以安全地用于在线调整决策策略。该框架通过价值函数学习、Double Q-learning目标和保守正则化，在保持运营约束的同时实现了策略的安全适应。

Abstract: Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.

</details>


### [148] [Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13607)
*Zach Studdiford,Gary Lupyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究比较了人类和25个大语言模型在日常情境常识推理任务上的表现,发现两者都存在相似的系统性错误模式。通过识别LLM中的注意力头发现这些头实现了某种模式匹配功能,且可以预测人类因无关提示细节导致的推理错误。研究结论认为人类和LLM的日常因果推理更符合模式匹配而非抽象世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界常用LLM的推理失败作为证据,认为其并非真正推理而是模式匹配,但同时认为人类行为不会表现出相同类型的失败,因为人类推理使用原则性的抽象世界模型。该研究试图验证这一假设是否成立。

Method: 1) 在多种日常情境的常识推理任务上同时评估人类参与者和25个LLM;2) 识别驱动LLM响应的注意力头集合;3) 利用这些注意力头预测人类看似莫名其妙的推理错误。

Result: 人类和LLM在日常常识推理中表现出相似的错误模式;驱动LLM响应的注意力头实现了某种形式的模式匹配;这些注意力头可以预测人类因表面上无关的提示细节而产生的推理错误。

Conclusion: 人类和LLM的日常因果推理都与模式匹配更为一致,而非抽象世界模型。

Abstract: When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.

</details>


### [149] [AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608)
*Xiaoyuan Liu,Jianhong Tu,Yuqi Chen,Siyuan Xie,Sihan Ren,Tianneng Shi,Gal Gantar,Evan Sandoval,Donghyun Lee,Daniel Miao,Peter J. Gilbert,Nick Hynes,Mauro Staver,Warren He,David Marn,Andrew Low,Xi Zhang,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Drouin,Alexandre Lacoste,Ramayya Krishnan,Elham Tabassi,Yu Su,Victor Barres,Chenguang Wang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA) 框架，通过让评判 agent 执行评估任务，并采用 A2A 和 MCP 等标准化协议，实现开放、agent 无关的评估体系。同时提出 AgentBeats 作为具体实现，包含五种操作模式。两大实证研究（298 个评判 agent 与 467 个被测 agent 的开放竞赛，以及编码 agent 案例研究）验证了该框架的覆盖性、实用性和保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent 评估基准存在碎片化问题：依赖固定的 LLM 中心化测试框架、需要 heavy 集成、存在测试-生产不匹配，且无法公平比较不同设计的 agent。核心痛点是缺乏开放、agent 无关的评估接口。

Method: 提出 AAA 框架：使用评判 agent 执行评估，所有参与者通过标准化协议交互——A2A 负责任务管理，MCP 负责工具访问。AAA 仅需一个接口（传统基准需要两个：基准接口和 agent 接口），从而实现通用统一框架，隔离评估逻辑与 agent 实现。提出 AgentBeats 作为 AAA 的具体实现，包含五种实用操作模式以适应开放性、隐私性和可重现性的实际约束。

Result: 两大实证研究验证了设计：1) 五个月的开放竞赛，12 个类别共 298 个评判 agent 和 467 个被测 agent 参与，证明 AAA 适用于异构基准；2) 编码 agent 案例研究，确认 agent 化评估在保持与公开记录一致性的同时，揭示了此前缺失的头对头比较结果，产出关于 agent 设计的 insights。

Conclusion: AAA 和 AgentBeats 为开放、标准化和可重现的 agent 评估提供了清晰路径，在异构场景下实现了覆盖性、实用性和保真度的统一。

Abstract: Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility.
  To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond Runtime Enforcement: Shield Synthesis as Defensibility Analysis for Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13621)
*Achraf Hsain,Sultan Almuhammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新定义了屏蔽强化学习的用途，将其从运行时安全机制转变为设计时的分析工具。通过构建一个两人安全博弈框架来评估网络防御能力，将时序逻辑规范编译为自动机，计算吸引子并提取获胜区域，从而得到形式化证书（可防御性判决）。除了二元判决外，还从吸引子结构中导出拓扑级指标，结合屏蔽约束的多智能体强化学习的收敛后行为，形成「可防御性指纹」，捕获网络的正式安全性和适应性博弈下的操作行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将屏蔽强化学习视为运行时安全机制，将时序逻辑规范编译为自动机来约束智能体的行动。作者认为这种定位是错误的——这种自动机理论工具应该被视为设计时的分析仪器，用于提供系统结构性洞察，而非对部署智能体的运行时约束。

Method: 构建一个受限的两人安全博弈框架：防守方规范定义博弈的不安全区域，攻击方规范在吸引子计算期间限制对手的合法行动。求解博弈得到可防御性判决（形式化证书）。同时从吸引子结构中导出拓扑级指标，结合屏蔽约束的多智能体强化学习的收敛后行为形成「可防御性指纹」。

Result: 可防御性指纹捕获了网络的形式化安全性和适应性博弈下的操作行为。「假设分析」表明形式化可防御性与操作有效性捕捉了安全性的不同方面：小型架构变更可能导致操作结果的大幅变化，而形式化安全边界几乎不变。

Conclusion: 屏蔽合成的最大价值不在于作为安全智能体的部署机制，而在于作为回答架构问题的框架：系统是否、在哪里以及如何能够被防御。可防御性判决是输出，而非安全策略本身。

Abstract: Shielded reinforcement learning is typically presented as a runtime safety mechanism that compiles temporal-logic specifications into automata restricting an agent's actions. We argue this is the wrong product. The same automata-theoretic machinery -- specification compilation, product game construction, attractor computation, and winning-region extraction -- is better read as a design-time analytical instrument whose outputs are structural insights about a system rather than runtime constraints on a deployed agent.
  We instantiate this through a constrained two-player safety game for network defense. The two specifications are enforced asymmetrically: the defender specification defines the unsafe region of the game, whereas the attacker specification restricts the adversary's legal actions during attractor computation. Solving the game yields a defensibility verdict -- a formal certificate that a topology-specification pair is or is not defensible -- with the associated winning region and shield.
  Beyond the binary verdict, we derive topology-level metrics from the attractor structure and combine them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained adversarial multi-agent reinforcement learning. Together these form a defensibility fingerprint capturing both a network's formal safety properties and its operational behavior under adaptive play.
  A what-if analysis shows that formal defensibility and operational effectiveness capture distinct aspects of security: small architectural changes can produce large shifts in operational outcomes while leaving formal safety margins nearly unchanged. Shield synthesis is thus most valuable not as a deployment mechanism for safe agents, but as a framework for answering architectural questions about whether, where, and how a system can be defended. The defensibility verdict is the output, not the safe policy.

</details>


### [151] [Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13658)
*Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini,Massimo Chiriatti,Enrico Panai,Giuseppe Riva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了理解人工智能认知和认识论影响的三种框架（Tri-System Theory、Thinkframes和System 0），认为System 0具有独特地位，因为它引入了"认知殖民化"概念，能够捕捉AI系统如何以用户难以察觉的方式将外部利益嵌入自我架构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人工智能已广泛部署，但现有框架难以完全解释AI对人类认知的隐蔽影响方式，因此需要一种能够揭示这种"不可见"影响机制的理论框架。

Method: 论文采用比较分析框架的方法，对三种理论进行系统性评估，并通过"认知殖民化"概念来阐述AI系统如何嵌入外部利益。

Result: 论文论证了System 0在理论上具有独特地位，能够捕捉Tri-System Theory和Thinkframes无法完全复制的认知影响维度。

Conclusion: 理解AI的"认知殖民化"效应是一项紧迫的哲学和实践任务，因为这些系统已在现实中广泛部署，其对个人推理和集体认知实践的影响需要深入研究。

Abstract: This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.

</details>


### [152] [Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13669)
*Zongsheng Cao,Bihao Zhan,Jinxin Shi,Jiong Wang,Fangchen Yu,Zhijie Zhong,Zijie Guo,Tianshuo Peng,Zhuo Liu,Yi Xie,Xiang Zhuang,Yue Fan,Runmin Ma,Shiyang Feng,Xiangchao Yan,Anran Liu,Peng Ye,Wenlong Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agents-K1是一个端到端的科学知识编排管道，通过多模态解析器、4B信息提取骨干网和图CLI界面，将原始文档转换为面向智能体的科学知识图谱。该系统处理了246万篇科学论文，构建了Scholar-KG，并在科学信息提取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理方面取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的研究智能体主要关注智能体编排，忽视了科学知识编排。现有工作将论文简化为摘要、表面提及和平坦的引用边，遗漏了对科学推理至关重要的关键实体、主张、证据、机制和方法谱系。

Method: Agents-K1由三个组件构成：(1)多模态解析器，采用五模块模式捕获实体、多模态证据、引用和全文级别的类型化实体间关系；(2)4B信息提取骨干网，使用GRPO在规则奖励下训练；(3)graphanything CLI，一种三源智能体接口，统一网络搜索、多模态图检索和跨文档遍历。基于此处理了246万篇科学论文。

Result: 构建了Scholar-KG知识图谱（发布100万篇论文子集），并展示了管道的可扩展性（通用领域语料库和模式一致数据合成）。实验表明Agents-K1在科学信息提取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理方面达到最优性能。

Conclusion: Agents-K1通过统一的理论框架和三大组件，实现了从原始文档到智能体原生科学知识图谱的端到端转换，为科学推理提供了更丰富的知识表示形式。

Abstract: Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [153] [Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13670)
*Tobias Holtdirk,Pietro Marcolongo,Anna Steinberg Schulten,Felix Henninger,Stefan Rose,Sarah Ball,Bolei Ma,Frauke Kreuter,Markus Weinmann,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This study demonstrates that LLMs can automate reproducibility assessments in social and behavioral sciences, achieving 96% qualitative conclusion agreement and 41% effect size recovery compared to human reanalysts' 74% and 34% respectively, showing promise as a scalable auditing tool.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reproducibility evaluation in social and behavioral sciences traditionally requires resource-intensive independent reanalysis by researchers, making it difficult to scale systematically across the literature.

Method: The researchers used 76 published studies with predefined claims from behavioral and social sciences. They compared LLM-generated analysis with both original findings and human reanalysis, evaluating effect size recovery using Cohen's d (±0.05 tolerance) and qualitative conclusion agreement.

Result: For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce viable effect size estimates. Among the remaining studies, the LLM pipeline recovered original effect sizes in 41% of cases and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 96% of cases. Human reanalysts recovered effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases.

Conclusion: Large language models can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.

Abstract: Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Eidola: Modeling Multi-GPU Network Communication Traffic in Distributed AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12638)
*Ranganath R. Selagamsetty,Matthew Poremba,Bradford M. Beckmann,Joshua San Miguel,Mikko H. Lipasti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Eidola，一个基于gem5的可扩展模拟框架，用于详细建模多GPU系统中的GPU间通信流量，解决现有工具难以处理内核融合和通信计算重叠技术带来的不规则、瞬态流量模式的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式AI工作负载规模增长，多GPU系统成为训练大型模型的必备设施。虽然内核融合和通信计算重叠等技术有助于减少延迟，但它们引入了不规则和瞬态的流量模式，难以使用现有工具进行建模。这些技术高度依赖细粒度同步和对等通信，对互连带宽和延迟造成巨大压力。

Method: Eidola是gem5模拟框架的可扩展扩展，采用“幻影”方法作为简洁的替身，仅模拟流量建模所需的最少特征。它使用真实应用的带注释时间配置文件，以周期级精度模拟对等GPU写入，支持可配置的每GPU流量模式和大规模多GPU配置的同步行为分析。

Result: 通过复现融合内核执行的变异性，验证了Eidola的有效性。实现了类似SyncMon的同步机制，确认了轮询相关内存流量减少。

Conclusion: Eidola提供了一个灵活且可扩展的平台，用于研究GPU间通信，支持现代分布式GPU系统的架构探索。

Abstract: As distributed AI workloads grow in scale, multi-GPU systems have become essential for training large models. Although techniques like kernel fusion and overlapping communication with computation help reduce delays, they also introduce irregular and transient traffic patterns that are difficult to model using existing tools. These techniques rely heavily on fine-grained synchronization and peer-to-peer communication, which place significant pressure on interconnect bandwidth and latency.
  In this work, we introduce Eidola, a scalable extension to the gem5 simulation framework that enables detailed modeling of inter-GPU communication traffic. The extension is scalable as our GPU model serves as a succinct eidolon, emulating the minimal characteristics needed for traffic modeling. Eidola uses annotated timing profiles from real applications to emulate peer-to-peer GPU writes with cycle-level precision. This allows researchers to simulate and analyze synchronization behavior across large multi-GPU configurations. The simulator supports configurable per-GPU traffic patterns and enables isolated performance analysis under different communication scenarios.
  We demonstrate Eidola's effectiveness by reproducing variability in fused kernel execution and by implementing a SyncMon-inspired synchronization mechanism, confirming reductions in polling-related memory traffic. Our results show that Eidola provides a flexible and scalable platform for studying inter-GPU communication and supports architectural exploration in modern distributed GPU systems.

</details>


### [155] [On the Limits of Performance Portability in Directive-Based GPU Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12753)
*Alessandro Romeo,Nitin Shukla,Stefano Truzzi,Alessio Suriano,Andrea Mignone*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究通过将天体物理磁流体动力学代码gPLUTO从OpenACC迁移到OpenMP，评估 directive-based GPU编程的性能可移植性。在NVIDIA A100上两种方案性能相当，但AMD MI250X上OpenMP实现慢了约3倍，关键瓶颈是内存访问模式和编译器限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学应用向GPU加速百亿亿次系统迁移面临性能、可移植性和生产率之间的权衡，需要评估不同指令式编程模型的跨架构表现。

Method: 将gPLUTO代码从OpenACC迁移到OpenMP，在NVIDIA A100 (Leonardo Booster)和AMD MI250X (LUMI-G)上进行基准测试，使用kernel级profiling分析性能差异根源。

Result: NVIDIA平台上OpenACC和OpenMP性能相当；AMD MI250X上OpenMP整体慢约3倍，kernel级慢速可达一个数量级。内存延迟bound的kernel和C++抽象层导致的寄存器溢出是主要因素，在低并行度场景下最坏慢47倍。

Conclusion: 跨GPU架构的便携性能不仅需要应用层修改，还需要编译器后端持续改进和架构感知优化策略。

Abstract: The transition of scientific applications to GPU-accelerated exascale systems is constrained by trade-offs between performance, portability, and productivity. This work evaluates the performance portability of directive-based GPU programming by porting gPLUTO, a production-grade magnetohydrodynamics code for astrophysical simulations, from OpenACC to OpenMP, and analyzing its performance on NVIDIA A100 (Leonardo Booster) and AMD MI250X (LUMI-G) devices. On NVIDIA platforms, OpenACC and OpenMP achieve comparable performance due to a shared compiler backend, providing a consistent baseline for assessing algorithmic efficiency. In contrast, the same OpenMP implementation is approximately three times slower at the application level on AMD MI250X with respect to the NVIDIA A100 OpenACC baseline, with kernel-level slowdowns reaching up to an order of magnitude, driven by sensitivity to strided memory-access patterns and compiler limitations. Kernel-level profiling shows that the dominant contributors to run-time are memory-latency-bound rather than limited by peak band-width. In low-parallelism kernels, C++ abstraction layers increase register pressure and spilling, leading to extreme slowdowns of up to 47x in specific cases. These results indicate that portable performance across GPU architectures requires not only application-level changes but also continued advances in compiler backends and architecture-aware optimization strategies

</details>


### [156] [SemanticXR: Low Power and Real-time Queryable Semantic Mapping with an Object-Level Device-Cloud Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12849)
*Rahul Singh,Devdeep Ray,Connor Smith,Sarita Adve*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SemanticXR是首个设备-云端协同的实时开放词汇语义建图系统，专为XR设备设计。通过将语义对象作为通信、执行和内存的一级单元，实现了服务器端2.2倍延迟提升、设备端500MB内存支持十万级对象、2.5Mbps上行带宽和仅2%功耗增加的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义建图方法计算密集，需要服务器级资源。云计算卸载是可行路径，但尚无系统能够在设备-云边界上合理分割语义建图任务并管理通信、执行和内存占用。

Method: 核心创新是将语义可识别对象提升为跨设备和服务器通信、执行和内存的一级单元。服务器端采用对象级并行化和几何降采样提升建图延迟，对象级深度建图协同设计减少上行带宽。设备端采用对象级稀疏本地地图、增量更新和更新优先级调度实现网络鲁棒的查询。提供可配置的资源使用与质量权衡机制。

Result: 与使用相同感知模型的设备-云基线相比，对象级组织将服务器建图延迟提升2.2倍。深度建图协同设计将上行带宽控制在2.5Mbps以下。设备端即使在网络中断下也能对最多10,000个对象维持亚100ms查询延迟，在500MB内存内支持数万个对象，下行带宽随地图变化而非场景规模缩放。系统仅增加2%设备功耗。

Conclusion: SemanticXR成功实现了XR设备上的实时开放词汇语义建图与查询，在严格功耗、带宽和内存约束下保持高质量语义理解。对象级抽象为移动端语义感知提供了可扩展的系统架构。

Abstract: Semantic mapping is a core service that enables grounded interactions in emerging Extended Reality (XR) applications such as AI assistants and spatial object search. Deploying this capability on mobile XR devices requires a system that is open-vocabulary, real-time, and low-power. Existing approaches are compute-intensive and assume server-class resources. Cloud offloading offers a practical path, but no existing system splits semantic mapping across the device-cloud boundary or manages its communication, execution, and memory footprint.
  We present SemanticXR, the first device-cloud system for real-time, open-vocabulary semantic mapping and querying under XR power, bandwidth, and memory constraints. Our key insight is to elevate semantically identifiable objects to first-class units of communication, execution, and memory across the device and server. On the server, object-level parallelism and geometry downsampling improve mapping latency, while object-level depth-mapping co-design reduces upstream bandwidth. On the device, an object-level sparse local map with incremental updates and update prioritization enables network-robust querying with bounded memory and downstream bandwidth. Object-level configurable resource usage vs. quality trade-offs let applications and the system adapt mapping to application requirements and operating conditions, respectively.
  Against a device-cloud baseline with the same perception models, object-level organization improves server-side mapping latency by 2.2X at equal semantic quality. Depth-mapping co-design maintains upstream bandwidth under 2.5 Mbps. On the device, SemanticXR sustains sub-100 ms query latency for up to 10,000 objects even under network drops, supports tens of thousands of objects within 500 MB, and scales downstream bandwidth with map changes, not total scene size. The system adds only 2% device power during normal operation.

</details>


### [157] [High-Order Spectral Element Methods for Wave Propagation on ARM Multicore CPU with SME: Optimizations and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12850)
*Yinuo Wang,Lin Gan,Tianqi Mao,Wubing Wan,Zekun Yin,Wenqiang Wang,Wei Xue,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对ARM多核处理器上的谱元法(SPECFEM3D)波传播计算进行了Scalable Matrix Extension(SME)优化，在LX2处理器上实现了4-6倍性能提升，并发现SME技术使高阶多项式成为更具性能优势的离散化选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SEM实现与支持SME扩展的新型ARM多核处理器架构不匹配，需要针对新硬件特性进行优化以提升波传播模拟效率。

Method: 提出三种优化策略：1) SME感知的批处理小矩阵核用于SEM张量积算子；2) 面向有限HBM系统的内存感知型混合MPI+OpenMP执行方案；3) 基于色散的(h,p)权衡等精度分析研究。

Result: 在固定多项式阶数下，优化实现相比原始代码全应用性能提升4-6倍，明显优于优化的非SME CPU基线。

Conclusion: SME不仅提升内核效率，还改变了ARM多核平台上SEM的实用离散化权衡，性能优势点向更高多项式阶数方向移动，从而进一步降低求解时间和内存工作集。

Abstract: Wave propagation based on the spectral element method (SEM) is a representative HPC workload, but existing SEM implementations are not well matched to emerging ARM multicore CPUs with Scalable Matrix Extension (SME). We present an SME-enabled optimization of \textsc{SPECFEM3D} on the emerging LX2 processor that combines an SME-aware batched small-matrix kernel for SEM tensor-product operators, a memory-aware hybrid MPI+OpenMP execution scheme for limited-HBM systems, and a dispersion-based iso-accuracy study of the $(h,p)$ tradeoff. At fixed polynomial order, the optimized implementation improves full-application performance by 4--6$\times$ over the original code and delivers clear gains over optimized non-SME CPU baselines. Beyond these implementation-level gains, our results suggest that SME shifts the performance-favorable operating point toward higher polynomial orders along the dispersion-based iso-accuracy frontier, further reducing time-to-solution and working-set size. These results indicate that SME affects not only kernel efficiency, but also the practical discretization tradeoff for SEM on modern ARM multicore platforms.

</details>


### [158] [Maestro: Workload-Aware Cross-Cluster Scheduling for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12950)
*Jinghao Wang,Xiao Zhou,Xiaoyang Sun,Yihui Zhang,Yilong Li,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Maestro是一个针对LLM多智能体系统(LLM-MAS)的工作负载感知调度系统，在GPU资源受限环境下通过预测输出长度和内存使用来驱动分层调度。实验表明可减少67.2%的KV保留HBM占用，并将高竞争场景下的SLO达成率提升23.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-MAS通过协作工作流处理复杂任务，但部署规模面临严峻挑战：每个用户查询触发迭代式LLM调用管道，资源消耗被大幅放大。在资源受限的云环境中，面临解码阶段非确定性和输入依赖的成本、多模型需求的内存碎片化与过度配置、以及跨集群调度权衡等问题。

Method: Maestro利用智能体语义和角色信息，显式预测每个阶段的输出长度和内存使用，驱动三层调度器：1) 节点层面通过分层权重缓存和弹性内存供给实现动态多模型共置；2) 集群层面进行延迟感知路由避免冷启动延迟和内存过载；3) 全局层面进行工作流感知优先级调度以减少交互式任务的头部阻塞。

Result: 原型实验和_trace驱动模拟表明：KV保留HBM减少67.2%，高竞争场景下SLO达成率相比EDF提升23.6个百分点。

Conclusion: Maestro通过工作负载感知和分层调度策略，有效解决了LLM-MAS在GPU资源约束下的高效服务问题，显著降低了内存占用并提升了服务质量保证。

Abstract: Large Language Model based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for tackling complex tasks by breaking them into collaborative workflows of specialized LLM-powered agents. However, deploying such multi-agent workloads at scale poses significant system challenges. Each user query spawns an iterative pipeline of LLM calls, greatly amplifying resource consumption compared to single-turn queries. In resource-constrained cloud settings, these workflows face non-deterministic and input-dependent costs at decode stage, heavy-tailed multi-model requirements with memory fragmentation and over-provisioning, and cross-cluster scheduling trade-offs. We present Maestro, a workload-aware scheduling system designed for LLM-MAS serving under strict GPU budgets. Maestro explicitly leverages agent semantics and roles: it predicts the output length and memory usage of each stage and uses this prediction to drive a hierarchical scheduler. At the node level, Maestro enables dynamic multi-model co-location via hierarchical weight caching and elastic memory provisioning. At the cluster level, it performs latency-aware routing to avoid cold-start delays and memory overloads. At the global level, it enforces workflow-aware prioritization to minimize head-of-line blocking for interactive tasks. Across prototype experiments and trace-driven simulations, Maestro reduces KV-reservation HBM by 67.2% and improves high-contention SLO attainment over EDF by 23.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [159] [JiRAIYA: A Reputation-Based Hierarchical Federated Learning Framework on Web3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13180)
*Venkata Raghava Kurada,Pallav Kumar Baruah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种利用Web3技术增强联邦学习透明度、可信性和可审计性的新框架，采用分层架构和共识机制防御对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习系统主要部署在企业环境中，存在透明度不足和审计受限的问题。聚合过程不透明，且当前缓解策略依赖外部验证器，带来额外计算和通信开销。

Method: 提出基于Web3的联邦学习框架，采用分层架构（委托管理器编排各联邦训练）；结合新颖性检测和共识机制抵御对抗/投毒攻击；模型更新广播给所有管理器，独立评估有效性，经共识批准的更新才纳入全局模型；采用基于信誉分数的备份机制确保模型生成。

Result: 在真实场景下的广泛实验证明了所提框架的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架有望在传统企业环境之外实现透明的联邦学习。

Abstract: Federated Learning(FL) is predominantly deployed in enterprise environments, where limited transparency and restricted auditability hinder broader adoption. Existing FL systems often suffer from opaque aggregation processes, making it unclear which model updates are accepted or discarded. Current mitigation strategies typically rely on external validators introducing additional computational and communication overhead. In this paper, we propose a novel FL framework that leverages existing Web3 technologies to enhance transparency, trust and auditability throughout the training process. The framework adopts a hierarchical architecture in which delegated managers orchestrate the FL training process within their respective federations. To mitigate adversarial and poisoning attacks, a combination of novelty detection and consensus mechanisms were employed. Model updates are encoded and broad casted to all managers, who independently evaluate their validity and those model updates that are approved by the consensus are incorporated into the global model. Additionally, a reputation score based backup mechanism is employed to ensure model generation. Extensive experiments conducted under real world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness, resilience of the proposed framework, highlighting its potential to enable transparent FL beyond traditional enterprise setting.

</details>


### [160] [Dynamic Resource Management in Production HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13266)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Guillaume Houzeaux,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用的MPI可延展性(Malleability)方法，在无需修改代码或调度器的情况下，实现HPC资源的动态管理。通过DMR框架集成到两个大规模科学应用中，在三台TOP500超级计算机上验证，能在不降低性能的前提下显著减少节点小时消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生产级HPC集群仍依赖固定资源配置，而大多数动态调整技术仍停留在实验室原型阶段，缺乏与实际生产环境软件的集成方案，导致可延展性技术难以推广应用。

Method: 提出DMR框架，实现了与主流HPC软件栈和运维实践的集成。采用非侵入式设计，无需对现有应用代码或调度器进行修改。提供DMR API供应用程序调用。

Result: 在TOP500超级计算机的生产配置下验证，非侵入式可延展性方案在受控环境中达到与静态基线相当的性能，同时为相同工作负载显著减少了节点小时消耗。

Conclusion: 可延展性技术可以在使用标准资源管理器的生产系统上有效利用，为降低HPC动态资源管理的应用门槛提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Many large-scale scientific applications exhibit time-varying behavior, yet production HPC clusters still rely on rigid, fixed-size allocations, and most dynamic techniques remain confined to laboratory prototypes. This work presents a practical MPI malleability methodology that integrates with state-of-the-art high-performance computing (HPC) software stacks and operational practices. The methodology is implemented in the Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) framework and is designed to ease adoption by existing applications without requiring intrusive code changes or scheduler modifications. We evaluate our approach by integrating the DMR API into two large-scale scientific applications and deploying them on three TOP500 supercomputers under realistic production configurations. Our non-invasive malleability solution achieves performance comparable to static baselines in controlled environments while substantially reducing node-hour consumption for identical workloads. These results show that malleability can be effectively exploited on production systems using vanilla resource managers, lowering the barrier to adoption of dynamic resource management in HPC.

</details>


### [161] [Skiplists with Foresight: Skipping Cache Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13321)
*Tomer Cory,Niv Sulimany,Erez Petrank*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Foresight是一种缓存友好的skiplist优化方法，可将顺序和并发skiplist的吞吐量提高45%，在DBx1000内存数据库中实现15%的端到端性能提升。该优化易于集成到现有skiplist设计中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Skiplist是系统应用中广泛使用的基础数据结构，但存在缓存不友好的问题，需要针对现代计算机架构进行优化，特别是在并发场景下面临同步挑战。

Method: 提出Foresight优化方法，通过外科手术式（surgical）的精准优化提升skiplist的缓存友好性。该优化可应用于顺序skiplist和三种并发skiplist设计实现。

Result: 微基准测试中吞吐量提升最高达45%；在DBx1000内存数据库的基于skiplist的索引中，端到端性能提升达15%。

Conclusion: Foresight优化方法效果显著，易于集成到各类skiplist设计中，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: A skiplist is a fundamental data structure widely used in systems and applications for indexing data stores. In this work, we introduce Foresight, a cache-friendly skiplist optimization. Extending Foresight to concurrent settings introduces significant synchronization challenges that we identify and address. Foresight is a surgical optimization, easy to integrate into a wide variety of skiplist designs. We apply it to one sequential and three concurrent skiplist designs and observe throughput improvements of up to 45% in microbenchmarks. When applied to a skiplist-based index in the DBx1000 in-memory database, Foresight yields end-to-end performance gains of up to 15%.

</details>


### [162] [Work Stealing for the 2D-Mesh Topology of Satellite Constellations in Low Earth Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13329)
*Mia Reitz,Dorian Chenet,Jonas Posner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出了针对太空边缘计算( SEC )的邻域仅限工作窃取策略,workers仅从直接相连的邻居窃取任务,避免多跳通信。分析模型表明该策略在大规模星座中每次窃取延迟优势随规模增长而增加。HPC集群实验显示,在平衡和不平衡工作负载下,邻域策略性能与全局窃取相当(差距~2.2%),表明限制窃取集合不会损害负载均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AMT运行时假设全连接低延迟网络,但LEO卫星星座形成稀疏网状拓扑,通信需多跳且每次增加延迟。现有全局工作窃取策略不适用于此类稀疏网络环境,需要设计适应空间网络的AMT工作窃取机制。

Method: 1) 提出邻域仅限工作窃取策略,workers仅从直接相连邻居窃取任务; 2) 建立分析模型量化不同策略的延迟优势; 3) 在HPC集群上通过模拟网状拓扑和低延迟链路进行初步实验验证。

Result: 分析模型显示限制窃取范围在星座规模增大时每次尝试的延迟优势随之增长。HPC实验表明在平衡和不平衡工作负载下,邻域策略性能与全局窃取差距在~2.2%以内。

Conclusion: 邻域仅限窃取可与全局窃取性能相当,且随着规模增大成为更优选择。这为将AMT模型适应太空边缘计算环境奠定了基础。

Abstract: Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) is a parallel programming model used in High Performance Computing (HPC). An AMT runtime can distribute fine-grained tasks across processing units called workers, through work stealing: when a worker has no tasks left to process, it tries to steal tasks from other workers. Workers are not restricted to a single compute node but can also be distributed across multiple nodes of an HPC cluster. Existing AMT runtimes assume a fully connected network with low, uniform latency and perform global work stealing, selecting another worker at random from all workers in the system.
  Space Edge Computing (SEC) uses constellations of satellites in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) as distributed compute clusters. Unlike HPC clusters, LEO satellites communicate through inter-satellite links that form a sparse mesh topology. Reaching a distant satellite requires multiple hops, each adding latency.
  As a step toward adapting AMT to SEC, this paper proposes a neighbor-only work stealing strategy in which workers steal exclusively from directly connected neighbors, avoiding multi-hop communication. An analytical model shows that restricting stealing this way yields a per-attempt latency advantage that grows with constellation size. Preliminary experiments on an HPC cluster with an emulated mesh over uniform low-latency links isolate the effect of victim selection: the neighbor-only strategy performs within ~2.2% of global stealing on both balanced and irregular workloads, indicating that restricting the victim set does not harm load balancing in this setting. Taken together, the experiments suggest that neighbor-only stealing can be on a par with global stealing, and the model suggests that neighbor-only stealing becomes preferable at scale.

</details>


### [163] [Temporal Conductance and Bounds on the Voter Model for Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13374)
*Tatiana Rocha Avila,Holger Dell,John Lapinskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文引入时序电导( temporal conductance)作为动态网络的新连通性度量,推广了Berenbrink等人的 voter model收敛时间界从静态电导到时序电导,证明预期共识时间上界为O(m/(d_min × Φ)),并证明该界在常数因子内紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态电导在分析动态网络时存在缺陷:只要任意时刻快照断开,电导即为0,无法刻画跨时间的连通性。而voter model在动态网络上运行时,边在不同时间出现可以提供有效的意见传播路径,需要更精细的连通性度量。

Method: 定义时序电导Φ,使其能够捕获跨时间边的连通性;将Berenbrink等人基于静态电导的分析框架推广到时序版本,建立时序网络上的潜在函数进行分析。

Result: 证明voter model在动态网络上的预期共识时间为O(m/(d_min × Φ));通过构造下界实例证明该界在常数因子内tight。

Conclusion: 时序电导是分析动态网络动力学的有效工具,可推广至其他时序网络过程和时间非齐次马尔可夫链的研究。

Abstract: The voter model is a classical stochastic process that models how opinions might spread through a network: at each step, every node lazily adopts the opinion of a random neighbour; eventually all nodes share the same opinion (consensus). Stronger connectivity should yield faster consensus. Berenbrink, Giakkoupis, Kermarrec, and Mallmann-Trenn (ICALP 2016) make this precise via the network's conductance: if the network has $m$ edges, minimum degree $d_{\min}$, and conductance at least $φ$, then the voter model reaches consensus in expected $O(m/(d_{\min}φ))$ steps. Their results extend to dynamic networks with fixed vertex degrees by considering the network's conductance at each time step.
  We introduce temporal conductance $Φ$, a more general connectivity measure for dynamic networks. Unlike static conductance, which collapses to $0$ whenever some snapshot is disconnected, $Φ$ captures connectivity through edges that appear at different times. We generalise the results of Berenbrink et al. from static conductance to temporal conductance, showing that the expected consensus time of the standard voter model is at most $O(m/(d_{\min}Φ))$. Moreover, we prove that this bound is tight up to constant factors. We expect temporal conductance to be a useful primitive for analysing other dynamics on temporal networks, and potentially time-inhomogeneous Markov chains more generally.

</details>


### [164] [GF-DiT: Scheduling Parallelism for Diffusion Transformer Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13501)
*Xinwei Qiang,Yifan Hu,Shixuan Sun,Jing Yang,Han Zhao,Chen Chen,Yu Feng,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GF-DiT是一个面向Diffusion Transformer(Diffusion Transformers, DiTs)的弹性推理服务运行时系统，通过将GPU并行度作为可调度资源，动态调整运行请求的并行配置，从而解决静态并行策略导致的GPU利用率低和服务质量下降问题。该工作在vLLM-Omni中实现，在图像和视频扩散工作负载上实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着DiTs成为图像和视频生成的主导架构，对其高效服务的需求日益增长。然而，现有系统在整个请求生命周期内为每个请求分配固定的并行配置，这种静态并行策略无法适应DiT工作负载在请求间、执行阶段间和系统条件间的显著异质性，导致GPU利用率低和服务质量下降。

Method: GF-DiT提出两个核心设计：(1)异步执行抽象，将请求分解为独立可调度的轨迹任务，实现在线GPU重新分配；(2)无组集合通信(group-free collectives)，一种轻量级通信抽象，支持低开销的任意执行组在线Formation和重新配置。该系统在vLLM-Omni中实现。

Result: 相较于静态并行的固定流水线执行，GF-DiT实现了：(1)吞吐量提升最高6.01倍；(2)平均延迟降低最高95%；(3)SLO违规率降低最高90%；(4)通信组设置开销从778ms降低至约60μs。

Conclusion: 将GPU并行度作为一等可调度资源是实现高效DiT服务的有效方法。GF-DiT通过弹性并行调整机制，显著提升了DiT推理服务的性能、延迟和服务质量保障能力，同时大幅降低了通信开销，为未来高效生成式AI服务系统的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become the dominant architecture for image and video generation, creating growing demand for efficient DiT serving. Existing systems assign each request a fixed parallel configuration throughout its lifetime. However, DiT workloads exhibit substantial heterogeneity across requests, execution stages, and system conditions, making static parallelism inefficient and often leading to poor GPU utilization and degraded service quality.
  This paper argues that DiT serving should treat GPU parallelism as a first-class schedulable resource. We present GF-DiT, a policy-programmable runtime for elastic DiT serving that dynamically adapts the parallelism of running requests according to workload demands and service objectives. GF-DiT introduces an asynchronous execution abstraction that decomposes requests into independently schedulable trajectory tasks and enables online GPU reallocation. To make elastic parallelism practical, GF-DiT further proposes group-free collectives, a lightweight communication abstraction that supports low-overhead online formation and reconfiguration of arbitrary execution groups.
  We implement GF-DiT in vLLM-Omni and evaluate it on representative image and video diffusion workloads. Compared with fixed-pipeline execution with static parallelism, GF-DiT improves throughput by up to 6.01$\times$, reduces mean latency by up to 95%, lowers SLO violation rates by up to 90%, and reduces communication-group setup overhead from 778 ms to approximately 60 $μ$s.

</details>


### [165] [Finding Conservation Laws of Large Dynamical Systems with Tasks and Futures: A Case Study in Utilizing Dynamic Data Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13623)
*Rüdiger Nather*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出await_delete构造扩展future语义，实现安全的内存重用，并基于此开发了块对称矩阵求逆的未来驱动算法，在Taskflow中实现并在大型矩阵上展现近乎线性的强扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Future模型虽适合处理不规则算法中的细粒度数据依赖，但值不可变性限制了对高性能线性代数至关重要的内存回收能力。

Method: 引入await_delete构造扩展传统future语义，支持消费者完成后安全复用值；基于此扩展，在Taskflow中实现块对称矩阵求逆的未来驱动算法。

Result: 实验表明，未来机制在小规模问题上有显著开销，但在大型矩阵上实现近乎线性扩展，存在可摊销阈值使得未来机制成为大规模线性代数的高性能工具。

Conclusion: Futures通过await_delete扩展可成为大规模线性代数的高性能工具，适合大型问题规模。

Abstract: As parallel workloads grow in complexity, managing fine-grained data dependencies becomes a critical challenge. Futures offer a promising model for handling these dependencies, particularly in irregular algorithms, but they also come with the restriction of value-immutability. This immutability limits the ability to perform in-place memory updates, a necessity for high-performance linear algebra where memory recycling is paramount.
  In this paper, we address these limitations by introducing a new construct, await_delete, which extends traditional future semantics to allow safe value reuse once consumers are finished. Building on this extension, we present a novel future-based algorithm for the block-wise inversion of dense, symmetric matrices, motivated by a recent algorithm for finding conservation laws of dynamical systems.
  We implement our approach in an extended version of Taskflow and evaluate it through strong-scaling experiments. Our results demonstrate that while futures incur significant overhead on smaller problem sizes, they achieve nearly linear scaling on large matrices. We analyze the amortization threshold and show that futures are a viable high-performance tool for large-scale linear algebra.

</details>
